function getClosestPlayer(o, ...)
	local t = player[QA(419144406 % 6548610)] and player[QA(-865695 + 899061)]:FindFirstChild(QA(1114495279 % 5436399));
	if not t then
		return nil;
	end;

	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= QA(-676666 - (-710431)) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local o = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if o and (o[QA(39833 + -6467)] and o[QA(-393827 + 427193)]:FindFirstChild(QA(717642 - 684158))) then
			local t = o[QA(-124087 - (-157453))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(QA(424941909 % 12140237));
			if t and t[QA(411383 + -377976)] > 2170412220 % 8544930 then
				return o[QA(-598429 + 631795)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local Z, x = nil, math[QA(266517281 % 5438435)];
	for o, i in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if i ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[i[QA(-194243 - (-228187))]] and (i[QA(609230 + -575864)] and i[QA(251815911 % 16785503)]:FindFirstChild(QA(131814 - 98330)))) then
			local o = i[QA(584546386 % 4175093)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(QA(-924161 - (-957775)));
			if o and o[QA(-749976 - (-783383))] > 115936 - 115936 then
				local o = ((i[QA(348983 + -315617)][QA(831705 - 798221)][QA(-577615 + 611334)] - t[QA(-1000606 + 1034325)]))[QA(-914465 + 948099)];
				if o < x and o < L then
					x = o;
					Z = i[QA(223007 - 189641)];
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return Z;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for o, t in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if t and t[QA(716324 - 682346)] then
			t:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
